HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001052/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1969fdc227bfb83da4a8","text":"1. **The schemes are fully present within divine custody.** `ع ن د B004` supplies proximity and presence at something. The repeated `مَكْر` is placed `عِندَ ٱللَّه`; 14:42 denied heedlessness, 14:45 supplied clarity through examples, and 14:47 denies promise-failure. The packet supplies presence inside that knowledge-account sequence; I infer custody rather than spatial nearness alone. `وَعِندَ ٱللَّهِ مَكْرُهُمْ` reads as no scheme having an outside from which to evade reckoning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000217/B001","root_000217/B005","root_000655/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_27fbe5f8b711cada6388","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"14:46:10\",\"root_000655\",[\"B001\"]],[\"14:46:12\",\"root_000217\",[\"B001\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dağlar, insan düzeninin etkisini ölçen katı ve zor aşılır direnç birimidir; dünya ölçeğini gerçekten değiştiren ilahî eylemle aynı değildir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3d3a0b4d5e973c902032","text":"14:48 supplied the crucial correction: human schemes are only measured against moving solid masses, whereas divine action actually remaps earth and sky. 14:51's accounting then confirmed that “with God” assigns custody and eventual processing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001052/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_793cb855c6bdb5c32bd5","text":"1. **Plotting is bounded by presence even at mountain-moving scale.** The packet's accepted `م ك ر` inventory lacks the ordinary plotting branch; the fixed repeated construction itself supplies deliberate scheme, while `ع ن د` `B004` gives presence/proximity, `ز و ل` `B001` departure from state, and `ج ب ل` `B001` high solid mass plus `B009` difficulty/prevention. I do not import an unattested `م ك ر` branch. Structurally, their scheme is repeated but then located “with God”; I infer this location encloses its reach. The ayah reads as maximal destabilizing capacity already held within a superior field of knowledge.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000655/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_880971b9d945b0511185","text":"2. **Human destabilization is measured against solid mass and divine transformation.** `ز و ل B001/B002` supplies departure and movement, while `ج ب ل B001` supplies a high solid mass and `ج ب ل B005` hardness that stops digging. The schemes are scaled to mountain movement; 14:44 had quoted a claim of no departure, and 14:48 actually transforms earth and heavens. The packet supplies attempted movement versus effected transformation; I infer a contrast between human reach and divine world-remapping. Mountains serve as the resistance measure of the scheme. No `م ك ر` branch is retained as an activated lexical reading: its accepted packet inventory contains calf-build, plant, pigment, irrigation, and spoiled-date branches, and this eleven-ayah window supplies no visible mechanism strong enough to attach any of them to the fixed forms. The repetition remains a structural cue, but forcing an inventory branch would hide the abductive gap.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000217/B004","root_000217/B012","root_000655/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b4e170ccf560add52977","text":"2. **Mountains can stand for formed resistance as well as matter.** `ج ب ل` `B004` is innate formed nature and `B012` chiefs like mountains; `ز و ل` `B001` permits removal from state. Earlier arrogant leaders route whole peoples into ruin. I infer a plot may seek to dislodge entrenched orders or towering authorities, not only physical mountains. This reading remains exploratory because the grammar favors the hyperbolic material image.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000217/B004","root_000217/B012"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ba73fe5d810af67e449a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"14:46:12\",\"root_000217\",[\"B004\",\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Dağlar, yerleşmiş düzenleri veya yüksek yöneticileri temsil ediyor olabilir; ancak dilbilgisel yapı öncelikle maddi büyüklük anlatır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001052/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bd6c4a9f18a46603671e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"14:46:4\",\"root_001052\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Düzenlerin Allah katında bulunması, hiçbir planın bilgi ve hesap alanının dışında kalamadığı tam bir gözetim ve muhafaza durumudur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c4cbfd692a7b5c2a844a","text":"Ayah 14:47 supplied the correction the plot image needs: destabilizing power may move what appears firm, but it cannot make the promised outcome fail to arrive.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
